theory RHTNRecovery

// Function signature and definition of the equational theory E

functions: fst/1, h/1, pair/2, pk/1, sign/2, snd/1, succ_stmt/3, true/0,
           verify/3, vresp/3
equations:
    fst(<x.1, x.2>) = x.1,
    snd(<x.1, x.2>) = x.2,
    verify(sign(x.1, x.2), x.1, pk(x.2)) = true

rule (modulo E) Register:
   [ Fr( ~skc ), Fr( ~skp ) ]
  --[ Reg( $A ), OnlyOnce( $A ), IdentityIs( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) )
  ]->
   [
   !LtkC( $A, ~skc ), !LtkP( $A, ~skp ), !Pk( $A, pk(~skc) ),
   !PkC( $A, pk(~skc) ), !PkP( $A, pk(~skp) ),
   !Kh( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ), Out( <pk(~skc), pk(~skp)> )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_Classical:
   [ !LtkC( $A, ~skc ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedClassical( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skc ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_PostQuantum:
   [ !LtkP( $A, ~skp ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedPQ( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skp ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recognise_Without_Meeting:
   [
   !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ), !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ), !Kh( $S, priorKh ),
   In( <newkey, result> )
   ]
  --[
  Recognised( $V, $S ), RecognisedFor( $V, $S, newkey ),
  RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, result ), RecognisedWithoutMeeting( $V )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh, newkey, result)>, ~vsc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh, newkey, result)>, ~vsp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Old_Key_Proof:
   [ !LtkC( $S, ~oldc ), !LtkP( $S, ~oldp ), In( newkey ), !Pk( $P, pkP ) ]
  --[ OldKeyProof( $S, newkey ), OldKeyProofFor( $S, newkey, $P ) ]->
   [
   Out( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~oldc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~oldp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Met:
   [ ] --[ PriorCounterparty( $V, $S ) ]-> [ !Met( $V, $S ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recovery_Meeting:
   [ !Met( $V, $S ), Fr( ~nk ) ]
  --[ RecoveryMeeting( $V, $S ), MeetingKey( $V, $S, ~nk ) ]->
   [ AtMeeting( $V, $S, ~nk ), Out( ~nk ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recognise:
   [
   !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ), !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ), !Kh( $S, khS ),
   AtMeeting( $V, $S, newkey )
   ]
  --[
  Recognised( $V, $S ), RecognisedFor( $V, $S, newkey ),
  RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, 'match' ),
  HonestlyRecognised( $V, $S, newkey )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <'recognise', $S, newkey, 'match', 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(khS, newkey, 'match')>, ~vsc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(khS, newkey, 'match')>, ~vsp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response:
   [
   !LtkC( $P, ~psk ), !Kh( $S, khOld ), !Kh( $V, khV ), In( <kmOld, kmV> ),
   In( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, provAc, provAp> ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, priorKh, provBc, provBp> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOld), khOld ), Eq( h(kmV), khV ),
  Eq( verify(provAc, <'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>,
             fst(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAp, <'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>,
             snd(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( priorKh, khOld ),
  Eq( verify(provBc, <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh, newkey, result)>,
             fst(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provBp, <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh, newkey, result)>,
             snd(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( result, 'match' ), Distinct( $V, $S ), Distinct( $P, $S ),
  Distinct( khOld, newkey ), RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, result ),
  ReliedOn( $P, $V, $S ), ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V, $S, newkey, result ),
  PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, newkey )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'recovered', $S, newkey>, ~psk) ) ]

restriction AtLeastOneMatchInTheBlock:
  "∀ r1 r2 #i.
    (BlockResults( r1, r2 ) @ #i) ⇒ ((r1 = 'match') ∨ (r2 = 'match'))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ r1 r2 #i.
    (BlockResults( r1, r2 ) @ #i) ⇒ ((r1 = 'match') ∨ (r2 = 'match'))"
  */

rule (modulo E) Transfer_Countersign:
   [
   !LtkC( $Old, ~oc ), !LtkP( $Old, ~op ), In( nodekey ), !Pk( $New, pkNew )
   ]
  --[ CountersignedFor( $Old, nodekey, $New ) ]->
   [
   Out( <'transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, ~oc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, ~op)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Transfer:
   [
   !LtkC( $New, ~nsk ), !Kh( $Old, khOldT ), In( kmOldT ),
   In( <'transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New, provC, provP> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOldT), khOldT ),
  Eq( verify(provC, <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, fst(kmOldT)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provP, <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, snd(kmOldT)),
      true
  ),
  Distinct( $Old, $New ),
  PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( $New, $Old, nodekey )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'adopted', nodekey, $New>, ~nsk) ) ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Patron_Accept_Transfer:
     [
     !LtkC( $New, ~nsk ), !Kh( $Old, khOldT ), In( kmOldT ),
     In( <'transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New, provC, provP> )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( h(kmOldT), khOldT ), Eq( z, true ), Eq( z.1, true ),
    Distinct( $Old, $New ),
    PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( $New, $Old, nodekey )
    ]->
     [ Out( sign(<'adopted', nodekey, $New>, ~nsk) ) ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. $New  = $New.21
       $Old  = $Old.22
       kmOldT
             = kmOldT.25
       nodekey
             = nodekey.26
       provC = provC.27
       provP = provP.28
       z     = verify(provC.27,
                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey.26, $Old.22, $New.21>, fst(kmOldT.25))
       z.1   = verify(provP.28,
                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey.26, $Old.22, $New.21>, snd(kmOldT.25))
    
    2. $New  = $New.27
       $Old  = $Old.28
       kmOldT
             = <x.51, x.52>
       nodekey
             = nodekey.32
       provC = provC.33
       provP = provP.34
       z     = verify(provC.33,
                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey.32, $Old.28, $New.27>, x.51)
       z.1   = verify(provP.34,
                      <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey.32, $Old.28, $New.27>, x.52)
    
    3. $New  = $New.31
       $Old  = $Old.32
       kmOldT
             = <x.59, pk(x.60)>
       nodekey

       z     = verify(provC.37,

       kmOldT

       provP = provP.38
       z     = true
       z.1   = verify(provP.38,

    5. $New  = $New.31
       $Old  = $Old.32
       kmOldT
             = <pk(x.59), pk(x.60)>
       nodekey
             = nodekey.36
       provC = sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey.36, $Old.32, $New.31>, x.59)
       provP = sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey.36, $Old.32, $New.31>, x.60)
       z     = true
       z.1   = true
  */

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Recovery:
   [
   !LtkC( $P, ~psk ), !Kh( $S, khOld ), !Kh( $V, khV ), !Kh( $V2, khV2 ),
   In( <kmOld, kmV, kmV2> ),
   In( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, provAc, provAp> ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, priorKh, provBc, provBp> ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, newkey2, result2, priorKh2, provB2c, provB2p> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOld), khOld ), Eq( h(kmV), khV ), Eq( h(kmV2), khV2 ),
  Eq( newkey2, newkey ), Distinct( $V, $V2 ), Eq( priorKh2, khOld ),
  Eq( verify(provB2c,
             <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh2, newkey2, result2)>, fst(kmV2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provB2p,
             <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh2, newkey2, result2)>, snd(kmV2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAc, <'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>,
             fst(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAp, <'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>,
             snd(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( priorKh, khOld ),
  Eq( verify(provBc, <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh, newkey, result)>,
             fst(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provBp, <'rhtn/1:verifier', vresp(priorKh, newkey, result)>,
             snd(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  BlockResults( result, result2 ), Distinct( $V, $S ), Distinct( $V2, $S ),
  Distinct( $P, $S ), Distinct( khOld, newkey ),
  ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V, $S, newkey, result ),
  ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V2, $S, newkey2, result2 ),
  ReliedOn( $P, $V, $S ), ReliedOn( $P, $V2, $S ),
  PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, newkey )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'recovered', $S, newkey>, ~psk) ) ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Patron_Accept_Recovery:
     [
     !LtkC( $P, ~psk ), !Kh( $S, khOld ), !Kh( $V, khV ), !Kh( $V2, khV2 ),
     In( <kmOld, kmV, kmV2> ),
     In( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, provAc, provAp> ),
     In( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, priorKh, provBc, provBp> ),
     In( <'recognise', $S, newkey2, result2, priorKh2, provB2c, provB2p> )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( h(kmOld), khOld ), Eq( h(kmV), khV ), Eq( h(kmV2), khV2 ),
    Eq( newkey2, newkey ), Distinct( $V, $V2 ), Eq( priorKh2, khOld ),
    Eq( z, true ), Eq( z.1, true ), Eq( z.2, true ), Eq( z.3, true ),
    Eq( priorKh, khOld ), Eq( z.4, true ), Eq( z.5, true ),
    BlockResults( result, result2 ), Distinct( $V, $S ), Distinct( $V2, $S ),
    Distinct( $P, $S ), Distinct( khOld, newkey ),
    ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V, $S, newkey, result ),
    ReliedOnResponse( $P, $V2, $S, newkey2, result2 ),
    ReliedOn( $P, $V, $S ), ReliedOn( $P, $V2, $S ),
    PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, newkey )
    ]->
     [ Out( sign(<'recovered', $S, newkey>, ~psk) ) ]
    variants (modulo AC)

restriction OneKeyPerName:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  */

restriction Equality:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  */

restriction VerifierIsNotSubject:
  "∀ V S #i. (Distinct( V, S ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(V = S))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ V S #i. (Distinct( V, S ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(V = S))"
  */

lemma forging_a_recognition_needs_both_verifier_halves:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey V #i.
    (((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
      (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ #k. CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #k))) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey V #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #k. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i))"
*/
simplify

lemma no_relied_on_verifier_is_the_subject:
  all-traces
  "∀ P V S newkey #i.
    ((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
     (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ⇒
    (¬(V = S))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P V S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧
  V = S"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, V, newkey ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
            case Register
            solve( ReliedOn( $P, $S, $S ) @ #i )
              case Patron_Accept_Recovery_case_1
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case Patron_Accept_Recovery_case_2
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
            case Register
            solve( ReliedOn( $P, $S, $S ) @ #i )
              case Patron_Accept_Recovery_case_1
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case Patron_Accept_Recovery_case_2
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
  solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        solve( ReliedOn( $P, $S, $S ) @ #i )
          case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma every_relied_on_response_was_signed_as_such:
  all-traces
  "∀ P V S newkey result #i.
    (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((∃ #b. (RecognisedAs( V, S, newkey, result ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P V S newkey result #i.
  (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedAs( V, S, newkey, result ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
       (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
  case case_1
  solve( ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i )
    case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !KU( h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ) @ #vk.21 )
            case c_h
            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~skc)
                   ) @ #vk.16 )
              case Old_Key_Proof
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                                vresp(h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>), newkey, 'match')>,
                               ~skc.1)
                     ) @ #vk.23 )
                case Recognise
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case c_sign
                solve( !KU( ~skc.1 ) @ #vk.30 )
                  case Compromise_Classical
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                qed
              qed
            next
              case c_sign
              solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.30 )
                case Compromise_Classical
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~skp)
                       ) @ #vk.20 )
                  case Old_Key_Proof
                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                                    vresp(h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>), newkey, 'match')>,
                                   ~skc.1)
                         ) @ #vk.24 )
                    case Recognise
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  next
                    case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */

lemma acceptance_requires_a_signed_match:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    (∃ V. ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, 'match' ) @ #i)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  ∀ V. (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, 'match' ) @ #i) ⇒ ⊥"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  next
    case case_2
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  qed
next
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
qed

lemma forging_a_recognition_needs_the_classical_half_too:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey V #i.
    (((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
      (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ #k. CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #k))) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey V #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧

lemma a_recovery_never_installs_the_prior_identity:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i #r.
    ((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
     (IdentityIs( S, newkey ) @ #r)) ⇒
    (⊥)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i #r.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (IdentityIs( S, newkey ) @ #r)"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>)
       ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $A, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $A, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
  solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Kh( $A, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      by contradiction /* from formulas */
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma recovery_completes_honestly:
  exists-trace
  "∃ P S newkey V #m #r #i.
    (((((((MeetingKey( V, S, newkey ) @ #m) ∧
          (HonestlyRecognised( V, S, newkey ) @ #r)) ∧
         (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
        (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)) ∧
       (#m < #r)) ∧
      (#r < #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ A #k. Compromised( A ) @ #k))) ∧
    (¬(∃ X #w. RecognisedWithoutMeeting( X ) @ #w))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P S newkey V #m #r #i.
  (MeetingKey( V, S, newkey ) @ #m) ∧
  (HonestlyRecognised( V, S, newkey ) @ #r) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i) ∧
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (#m < #r) ∧
  (#r < #i) ∧
  (∀ A #k. (Compromised( A ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ X #w. (RecognisedWithoutMeeting( X ) @ #w) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !Met( $V, $S ) ▶₀ #m )
  case Met
  solve( !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ) ▶₀ #r )
    case Register
    solve( !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ) ▶₁ #r )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, khS ) ▶₂ #r )
        case Register
        solve( AtMeeting( $V, $S, ~nk ) ▶₃ #r )
          case Recovery_Meeting
          solve( ReliedOn( P, $V, $S ) @ #i )
            case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
            solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
                case Register
                solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
                  case Register
                  solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, ~nk ) @ #i )
                    case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
                    solve( !KU( h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ) @ #vk.21 )
                      case c_h
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, ~nk, $P)>, ~skc)
                             ) @ #vk.16 )
                        case Old_Key_Proof
                        solve( !KU( ~nk ) @ #vk.13 )
                          case Recognise
                          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, ~nk, $P)>, ~skp)
                                 ) @ #vk.19 )
                            case Old_Key_Proof
                            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                                              vresp(h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>), ~nk, 'match')>,
                                             ~vsc)
                                   ) @ #vk.23 )
                              case Recognise
                              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                                                vresp(h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>), ~nk, 'match')>,
                                               ~skp.1)
                                     ) @ #vk.24 )
                                case Recognise
                                solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.25 )
                                  case Register
                                  solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.26 )
                                    case Register
                                    solve( !KU( pk(~vsc) ) @ #vk.27 )
                                      case Register
                                      solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.28 )
                                        case Register
                                        SOLVED // trace found
                                      qed
                                    qed
                                  qed
                                qed
                              qed
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma recognition_alone_insufficient:
  all-traces
  "¬(∃ P S newkey #i.
      ((((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
         (∃ V #b. Recognised( V, S ) @ #b)) ∧
        (¬(∃ #a. OldKeyProof( S, newkey ) @ #a))) ∧
       (¬(∃ #c. Compromised( S ) @ #c))) ∧
      (¬(∃ #kp. Compromised( P ) @ #kp)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∃ V #b. (Recognised( V, S ) @ #b)) ∧
  (∀ #a. (OldKeyProof( S, newkey ) @ #a) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #c. (Compromised( S ) @ #c) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #kp. (Compromised( P ) @ #kp) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register

                case Register

                        case c_h

                               ) @ #vk.21 )
                          case Old_Key_Proof
                          by contradiction /* from formulas */
                        next
                          case c_sign
                          solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.43 )
                            case Compromise_Classical
                            by contradiction /* from formulas */
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            next
              case Recognise_Without_Meeting
              solve( !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ) ▶₀ #b )
                case Register

                  case Register
                  solve( !Kh( $S, priorKh ) ▶₂ #b )
                    case Register
                    solve( !KU( h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ) @ #vk.26 )
                      case c_h

  next
    case case_2
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
            case Register
            solve( Recognised( V.1, $S ) @ #b )
              case Recognise
              solve( !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ) ▶₀ #b )
                case Register
                solve( !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ) ▶₁ #b )
                  case Register
                  solve( !Kh( $S, khS ) ▶₂ #b )
                    case Register

                        next

lemma recognition_binds_the_successor:
  all-traces
  "∀ P V S newkey result #i.
    (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
      ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
       (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P V S newkey result #i.
  (ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)))) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
       (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
  case case_1
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
    case case_1
    solve( ReliedOnResponse( P, V, S, newkey, result ) @ #i )
      case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
      solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !KU( h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ) @ #vk.21 )
              case c_h
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~skc)
                     ) @ #vk.16 )
                case Old_Key_Proof
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', 
                                  vresp(h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>), newkey, 'match')>,
                                 ~skc.1)
                       ) @ #vk.23 )
                  case Recognise
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next
                  case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next

                  qed

lemma successor_statement_binds_the_patron:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((∃ #a. (OldKeyProofFor( S, newkey, P ) @ #a) ∧ (#a < #i)) ∨
      ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
       (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #a. (OldKeyProofFor( S, newkey, P ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)))) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
       (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
  case case_1
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
    case case_1
    solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
      case Patron_Accept_Recovery
      solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
        case case_1
        solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
                case Register
                solve( !KU( h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ) @ #vk.26 )
                  case c_h
                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', succ_stmt($S, newkey, $P)>, ~skc)
                         ) @ #vk.21 )
                    case Old_Key_Proof
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  next
                    case c_sign
                    solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.43 )
                      case Compromise_Classical
                      by contradiction /* from formulas */
                    qed
                  qed
                qed

            qed

            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
                case Register
                solve( !KU( h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ) @ #vk.26 )

lemma transfer_statement_binds_the_destination:
  all-traces
  "∀ New Old nodekey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( New, Old, nodekey ) @ #i) ⇒
